### Thesis Notes Based on Today's Price Action
- **Three regime lessons (#2/#4/#5) all simultaneously validated today.** Today was the highest-conviction single-day validation of the structural-cash-cushion-as-cushion thesis since launch. The book OP'd +1.11pp on a day SPY closed −0.75% / Nasdaq −1.25% / AI-cohort-data-center-cost-worried / OpenAI-IPO-delay-headline — exactly the regime the book is engineered for.
- **LLY DOUBLE-RATCHET = peak post-confirmed-beat-hold lesson #3 payoff.** The +$299.38 unrealized P&L booked intraday today would not have existed if LLY had been trimmed during any of the 8 prior sessions' cushion-anxiety windows (6/22 day-1 3.14% cushion week-low etc). **Empirically:** patience-on-thesis-confirmed-winners is paying multi-bps of locked-in gain via mechanical stop ratchet. **Going forward:** the bar for manually tightening a winning trail past the 10% trail is even higher post-today's evidence.

### Thesis Notes Based on This Week's Price Action
- **The V add plan-vs-execution gap across 3 sessions was NOT a system failure — it was a plan-refinement sequence** where each successive research entry narrowed the criteria in response to changing tape character (Mon path (a) clean → Tue chase-rally reversal → Wed noisy-multi-macro confluence). **Forward:** Keep V as the primary Week-8 diversifier candidate; Mon 7/6 add gate reset with same mechanics; but do NOT assume the plan is self-executing without confirming the intra-day trigger points hit at Alpaca. **Also:** the LAPSE has been a net-neutral outcome in retrospect — V was at $336 Mon 6/29 pre-mkt; V likely closed Wed 7/1 in the $340–345 range on the Tue chase-rally + Wed drift, so Mon-execution would have been ~+1–3% into the book. Not a hero-move miss.
- **Lesson #2 (cash-cushion-as-cushion) got its first FULL BINARY-REGIME-PAIR empirical test across Weeks 6+7:** Week 6 DOWN-tape OP +2.46pp; Week 7 UP-tape UND −2.61pp WTD; net ~−0.15pp = essentially breakeven. **The structural cash-cushion vs SPY nets close to zero across a symmetric binary-regime pair — meaning the strategy's alpha MUST come from single-name selection, not from index-timing beta.** LLY's +$797.78 unrealized P&L is the alpha proof-point (single-name post-confirmed-beat-hold lesson #3).
- **Lesson #3 (post-confirmed-beat-hold) keeps compounding empirically.** LLY's 3-ratchet sequence in 2 sessions (Fri midday, Fri afternoon, Mon morning) captured mechanical HWM advances with zero manual interventions; the subsequent 3-session consolidation (−3.7% Mon HWM to Wed close) VINDICATED the do-not-manually-tighten call in real-time. Had Fri manual tighten to 7% happened, stop would sit at ~$1,151 today = ~3.4% cushion = tight on normal consolidation. **The 10% trail is doing exactly what it's designed to do.**
- **Lesson #4 (diversifier-on-right-regime) expanding:** ISRG delivered TWO validating sessions this week (Fri 6/26 AI-cohort-risk-off + Wed 7/1 semi-profit-taking rotation) — the diversifier payoff regime is expanding beyond just "hard AI-cohort risk-off" to include "modest rotation-into-defensives" sessions. Small but real expansion of the thesis payoff regime.
- **Lesson #1 pattern refinement (candidate for lessons-file add at weekly review):** When cushion is right at the 3% edge (not deep below like Thu 6/25's 2.49% or Tue 6/30's 2.49%), and the tape is modest-risk-off structurally (like Wed 7/1's pre-mkt fade of Tue's chase-rally), path (a) hold-the-stop dominates over path (b) manual-trim on cushion-alone. Wed ISRG's intraday +2.09% recovery from 3.00% edge to 4.49% close proves the case.
- **NFP-Thu is a rate-driven binary, not an AI-cohort binary.** Historical experience: rate-driven risk-off benefits LLY-pharma more than ISRG-medtech (Fri 6/26 double-ratchet came off hot-PCE + rate-hike-odds-reset regime; Mon 6/29 3rd-ratchet came off US-Iran-truce rally that continued the LLY momentum). **Hot-NFP scenario is where the book is best positioned:** LLY catches pharma-defensive bid + potential 4th ratchet; ISRG catches modest medtech-defensive bid; cash cushion structurally OP's the SPY risk-off. **Cool-NFP scenario is the risk case:** structural UND-day on top of Week 7's already-largest UND-week; cumulative UND could widen to −1.7 to −2.0pp; Week 7 WTD UND could deepen to −3.0 to −3.5pp.

- **The Week 7 UND (−2.61pp WTD through Wed close) is the largest structural UND-week of the cycle.** Netted against Week 6's +2.46pp record OP the binary-regime pair sits at ~−0.15pp = essentially breakeven. **Lesson #2 upgrade candidate confirmed: structural cash-cushion vs SPY nets close to zero across a symmetric binary-regime pair — strategy's alpha MUST come from single-name selection (lesson #3 post-confirmed-beat-hold on LLY), not from index-timing beta.**
